> DEVELOPER

And do you think that approach makes sense?

> AGENT

Yes, for this setup it makes sense.

A pure symlink would be cleaner only if every harness consumed the exact same file. Here they don’t:

- `megaplan/data/instructions.md` is the shared source.
- Claude gets shared source plus Claude-specific appendix.
- Codex gets shared source plus Codex-specific appendix.
- Cursor gets shared source in rule format.

So generated installed copies are reasonable. The important engineering rules are:

- Never hand-edit generated installed files as the durable fix.
- Put shared behavior in `megaplan/data/instructions.md`.
- Put harness-specific behavior only in the relevant appendix.
- Run `megaplan setup --force` to regenerate local installs.
- Commit the source, not the generated home-directory copies.

The weak spot is discoverability: the generated files don’t visibly say “do not edit; generated from X.” I’d add a generated-file header to the installed outputs so future agents don’t repeat my first mistake.

{
  "cmd": "megaplan cloud exec --cloud-yaml docs/megaplan/git-backed-packs/cloud.yaml 'cd /workspace/astrid && mkdir -p .megaplan/checkins && tee .megaplan/periodic-checkin.sh >/dev/null <<'\\''EOF'\\''\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -u\ncd /workspace/astrid\nSPEC=docs/megaplan/git-backed-packs/chain.yaml\nOUT_DIR=.megaplan/checkins\nmkdir -p \"$OUT_DIR\"\nwrite_checkin() {\n  local ts slug out current_plan state_file current_state last_step last_time latest_commit pr_line\n  ts=\"$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)\"\n  slug=\"$(date -u +%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ)\"\n  out=\"$OUT_DIR/checkin-$slug.md\"\n  current_plan=\"$(megaplan chain status --spec \"$SPEC\" 2>/dev/null | jq -r '.summary.current_plan_name // empty' 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n  state_file=\".megaplan/plans/$current_plan/state.json\"\n  current_state=\"\"\n  last_step=\"\"\n  last_time=\"\"\n  if [ -n \"$current_plan\" ] && [ -f \"$state_file\" ]; then\n    current_state=\"$(jq -r '.current_state // empty' \"$state_file\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n    last_step=\"$(jq -r '.history[-1].step // empty' \"$state_file\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n    last_time=\"$(jq -r '.history[-1].timestamp // empty' \"$state_file\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n  fi\n  latest_commit=\"$(git log --oneline -1 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n  pr_line=\"$(gh pr view 8 --json number,state,mergeable,headRefName,url 2>/dev/null | jq -r '\"#\\(.number) \\(.state) mergeable=\\(.mergeable) head=\\(.headRefName) \\(.url)\"' 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n  {\n    echo \"# Astrid Git-Backed Packs Check-In\"\n    echo\n    echo \"- Time: $ts\"\n    echo \"- Current plan: ${current_plan:-none}\"\n    echo \"- Current state: ${current_state:-unknown}\"\n    echo \"- Last phase: ${last_step:-unknown} ${last_time:+at $last_time}\"\n    echo \"- Latest commit: ${latest_commit:-unknown}\"\n    echo \"- PR: ${pr_line:-unknown}\"\n    echo\n    echo \"## Chain\"\n    megaplan chain status --spec \"$SPEC\" 2>&1 | sed -n '1,90p'\n    echo\n    echo \"## Active Processes\"\n    ps -eo pid,etime,pcpu,pmem,args | rg 'megaplan chain|megaplan auto|megaplan execute|megaplan review|megaplan plan|megaplan finalize|chain-one|chain-supervisor' || true\n    echo\n    echo \"## Git\"\n    git status --short --branch\n    echo\n    echo \"## Recent Chain Log\"\n    tail -80 .megaplan/cloud-chain-one-pr-push-each.log 2>/dev/null || true\n  } > \"$out\"\n  cp \"$out\" \"$OUT_DIR/latest.md\"\n  echo \"[$ts] wrote $out\" >> .megaplan/periodic-checkins.log\n}\nwrite_checkin\nwhile true; do\n  sleep 3600\n  write_checkin\ndone\nEOF\nchmod +x .megaplan/periodic-checkin.sh\ntmux kill-session -t megaplan-periodic-checkins 2>/dev/null || true\ntmux new-session -d -s megaplan-periodic-checkins /workspace/astrid/.megaplan/periodic-checkin.sh\nsleep 2\ntmux ls\nprintf \"\\n--- latest ---\\n\"\nsed -n \"1,180p\" .megaplan/checkins/latest.md\nprintf \"\\n--- log ---\\n\"\ntail -20 .megaplan/periodic-checkins.log'",
  "workdir": "/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/Astrid",
  "yield_time_ms": 1000,
  "max_output_tokens": 30000
}
